# Capability comparison

Finite supplied checks cover only their stated tested domains. Unverified behavior remains outside this evidence. Counts do not establish maintenance savings.

Status: "accepted"

Hypothesis: Share bilingual report rendering with translation data and preserved dispatch.

Categories are production, tests, fixtures, research support, configuration and dependency. Missing totals are unsupplied; they are never inferred as zero.
